# Encyclopedia implementation roadmap

**Updated:** 2026-10-26 (UTC).

## Current milestone

**Thirty-six implementation milestones are shipped across thirty-six live proposals.** Envelope KMS carries E-K-01's complete key-handle exercises and S02's key-evolving audit-log compromise lesson. Search Vault carries L-R-09's query-result integrity exercise, A-D-02's public-key searchable-encryption lesson, L-R-07's bounded Boolean-search lesson, E-K-08's fuzzy-search and similarity-leakage lesson and L-R-08's multi-user authorization, collusion and split-trust lesson. IBE Gate carries both E-K-02's executable HIBE delegation and A-D-07's bounded PKI/IBE/certificateless ownership comparison, AES Anatomy carries both A-D-06's classical differential fault analysis and E-K-06's accepted-output fault analysis, Dedup Vault delivers A-D-01's encrypted-deduplication lesson, Shelf Oracle carries L-R-04's compressed-query expansion plus one shared implementation for L-R-05's bounded stateful preprocessing PIR and A-D-10's executed cost model, Oblivious Shelf delivers L-R-06's bounded robust-PIR fault and collusion lesson, Attribute Gate carries both A-D-03's bounded hidden-value FABESA lesson and L-R-03's separately scoped RW15 multi-authority and application-layer epoch-gating lessons beside its unchanged FAME route, Proof Tally carries S04's bounded participant-contributed differential-privacy lesson beside its unchanged standardized Prio3 route, DC-net delivers A-D-04's anonymous-broadcast, coalition-view and failure-model lesson, HPKE Envelope delivers S01's executable Auth KCI and identity-bound Sign-then-Encrypt comparison, LFSR Forge delivers A-D-05's exact Boolean-combiner audit, A-D-11's separately scoped toy-oracle cube-sum and superpoly recovery and A-D-12's historical A5/1 majority-clocking/state-completion lesson, Clock Arithmetic delivers L-R-01's exact Montgomery REDC and encoded-chaining lesson, Hidden Bit delivers E-K-04's adjacent AES hybrids and bounded hard-core-predicate reconstruction plus S09's separate target-collision order games, IPsec / IKE delivers E-K-03's ordered SPD, IKEv2, directional Child-SA, ESP, replay, deletion and rekey lesson, Matsui Line delivers A-D-14's executable differential–linear composition and bounded whitening-byte ranking, Signature Gate delivers A-D-09's one-message designated-verifier proof and public-equation contrast, Residue Gate delivers A-D-13's repeated-squaring generator and Blum–Goldwasser rewind lesson, Attestation Gate delivers A-D-08's issuer-membership and controlled-linkability lesson, and Shamir Gate delivers S07's exact visual-sharing construction and complete one-share distribution proof. [Envelope KMS](https://systemslibrarian.github.io/crypto-lab-envelope-kms/) · [Search Vault](https://systemslibrarian.github.io/crypto-lab-search-vault/) · [IBE Gate](https://systemslibrarian.github.io/crypto-lab-ibe-gate/) · [AES Anatomy](https://systemslibrarian.github.io/crypto-lab-aes-anatomy/) · [Dedup Vault](https://systemslibrarian.github.io/crypto-lab-dedup-vault/) · [Shelf Oracle](https://systemslibrarian.github.io/crypto-lab-shelf-oracle/) · [Oblivious Shelf](https://systemslibrarian.github.io/crypto-lab-oblivious-shelf/) · [Attribute Gate](https://systemslibrarian.github.io/crypto-lab-attribute-gate/) · [Proof Tally](https://systemslibrarian.github.io/crypto-lab-proof-tally/) · [DC-net](https://systemslibrarian.github.io/crypto-lab-dc-net/) · [HPKE Envelope](https://systemslibrarian.github.io/crypto-lab-hpke-envelope/) · [LFSR Forge](https://systemslibrarian.github.io/crypto-lab-lfsr-forge/) · [Clock Arithmetic](https://systemslibrarian.github.io/crypto-lab-clock-arithmetic/) · [Hidden Bit](https://systemslibrarian.github.io/crypto-lab-hidden-bit/) · [IPsec / IKE](https://systemslibrarian.github.io/crypto-lab-ipsec-ike/) · [Matsui Line](https://systemslibrarian.github.io/crypto-lab-matsui-line/) · [Signature Gate](https://systemslibrarian.github.io/crypto-lab-confirmer-signatures/) · [Residue Gate](https://systemslibrarian.github.io/crypto-lab-residue-gate/) · [Attestation Gate](https://systemslibrarian.github.io/crypto-lab-attestation-gate/) · [Shamir Gate](https://systemslibrarian.github.io/crypto-lab-shamir-gate/)

LLL Break delivers E-K-09's historical Merkle–Hellman/Algorithm SV lesson, and MAC Race delivers L-R-02's exact byte-aligned Black–Rogaway 2002 PMAC lesson. [LLL Break](https://systemslibrarian.github.io/crypto-lab-lll-break/) · [MAC Race](https://systemslibrarian.github.io/crypto-lab-mac-race/)

A-D-07 is **live** at exact IBE Gate main `6c0b3fbd9ebf`. Its seventh exhibit compares a P-256-certified RSA-OAEP PKI route, the escrowed BasicIdent route and a bounded Huang–Wong-style IBE/PKE/MAC envelope in one fresh run. Type-I replacement and Type-II KGC capabilities fail separately and succeed only when deliberately combined; displacement denial, tag mutation, the unmet IND-CCA theorem premise, one-shot adversary scope, directory authentication and non-constant-time limits remain visible. Exact-main CI, deployment and byte-level public verification passed.

S06 is **live** at exact Distance Bounding main `4458307b57a0`. Its executable verifier-nonce Hancke–Kuhn profile keeps ordinary authenticated relay, timed full relay, mafia pre-asking and dishonest-prover distance fraud separate; exact integer/rational arithmetic derives every timing and probability result. Predictable challenges, nonce reuse, honest noise, repeated attempts, disabled timing and partial pre-asking are visible controls, while the page explicitly makes no browser-clock, radio-ranging or general relay-defense claim. Exact-main CI, CodeQL, deployment and byte-level public verification passed. [Distance Bounding](https://systemslibrarian.github.io/crypto-lab-distance-bounding/)

E-K-01 is **live**: Envelope KMS now searches a declared finite state model for mutable-role, known-key-import and distinct same-value-handle disclosures, distinguishes a completed bounded miss from an incomplete cutoff, and replays found witnesses with real RFC 3394 and AES-GCM. S02 is **live** there too: compromise immediately before period c exposes that period and the future, an earlier rewrite fails only in the key-evolving track under the stated one-way-evolution and modeled-erasure assumptions, and separate retained-checkpoint, expected-close and outside-heartbeat inputs keep integrity, consistency, completeness and freshness distinct. L-R-09 is **live**: Search Vault distinguishes per-record authenticity from completeness and freshness with honest, omission, fabrication and stale-snapshot cases. A-D-02 is **live** there too: a public sender creates randomized Type-3 BLS12-381 PEKS tags, the recipient issues a deterministic trapdoor, the server runs public Test, and a bounded attacker exercises exposed-trapdoor dictionary guessing while the interface keeps search, access, volume and active-guessing leakage separate. L-R-07 is now **live** in the same repository: two actual isolated SSE searches are compared with a bounded two-term OXT adapter over canonical ristretto255 points, five controls measure equality/intersection leakage, and the Path ORAM comparison keeps its separate-server assumptions explicit. E-K-02 is **live**: IBE Gate executes bounded descendant-key delegation, sibling/ancestor failure boundaries, subtree compromise and retained root escrow. A-D-06 is **live**: AES Anatomy derives last-round key constraints from real round-9 AES-128 faults, intersects candidates, reverses the key schedule and verifies the recovered master key. E-K-06 is also **live**: finite samples execute correct and first-round-faulted AES-128, classify exact ciphertext equality and rank one key byte from the selected inputs, while a separate analytic census, a secret-independent null and bounded infection/reference-oracle cases keep the statistical claim scoped. A-D-01 is **live**: Dedup Vault executes fresh-key, local message-locked and RFC 9497 VOPRF-aided modes, measures exact-byte deduplication, and keeps dictionary, role and lifecycle boundaries separate. L-R-04 is **live** in Shelf Oracle: four fixed compressed blocks expand to 64 selectors through executable automorphisms and key switching while the lesson keeps its full scan, four-response shape, circular-security assumption and non-claim of multidimensional or sublinear-server PIR explicit. L-R-05 is **live** there as a separate bounded Piano-style route: client setup streams the static database into private consumable hints, each online request probes eight records, and loss, malformed response, rollback and update cases fail closed or rebuild. A-D-10 is **live** through the same executable ledger, which keeps setup, retained state, wire bytes, records touched, parity contributions, refresh and rebuild costs in separate units rather than claiming a universal speedup. A-D-03 is **live**: Attribute Gate runs a separate lesson using Figure 5's CP-ABE equations with §3.3's partially hidden CP-A2BE payload over Type-3 BLS12-381, compares equal-public-payload worlds with different hidden values, enumerates bounded reconstruction candidates and recognizes a recovered group element only through AES-GCM tag acceptance. L-R-03 is **live** there too: exact asymmetric RW15 independently sets up Hospital and Board authorities, binds issued components to one canonical GID, executes same-GID success, raw cross-GID cryptographic failure and one-issuer compromise, while a separate FAME-based application adaptation executes complete-key epoch reissue, retained old access, trusted-checkpoint rollback detection and owner-assisted DEK rewrap. S04 is **live**: Proof Tally first subtracts two equal-size real Prio3Sum releases to expose a zero-replaced bounded contribution, then runs one all-or-nothing participant-noised release with explicit clipping, adjacency, exact discrete-Laplace sampling, threshold, dropout, collusion, cap and repeat boundaries. S01 is **live**: HPKE Envelope executes RFC 9180 Auth's recipient-key KCI forgery and compares it with a bounded strict-Ed25519 Sign-then-Encrypt profile whose identities, independently expected application context, compromise matrix and transferable-evidence limits are explicit. A-D-05 is **live**: LFSR Forge exhaustively analyzes an editable four-input Boolean function and executes bounded parity, pair-state and single-state counterexamples while keeping independent-uniform Walsh claims separate from measured deterministic LFSR agreement. A-D-11 is **live** there too: a separate exact toy oracle exhausts four two-variable cube faces, recovers their superpolys, uses three independent affine maxterms to filter eight keys to one and verifies the frozen result on disjoint held-out rows, while the UI states that it executes no Trivium state or round. A-D-12 is **live** in the same lab: the reverse-engineered historical A5/1 path reproduces setup checkpoints and both BGW bursts, contrasts majority with regular clocking and independently verifies a bounded leaked-state completion without claiming Kc recovery or a modern cellular attack. L-R-01 is **live**: Clock Arithmetic executes power-of-two Montgomery REDC on exact BigInts, exposes cancellation and final subtraction, chains encoded products and squares, rejects invalid parameters and separates setup, ordinary-loop, Montgomery-loop and final-decoding measurements without a speedup or constant-time claim. A-D-09 is **live**: Signature Gate executes the paper's concrete section 5 trapdoor-commitment construction over its existing Type-3 relation, accepts one proof from the branch witness and another from the verifier secret, exposes a false-statement simulation and compares both with a separately bounded public equation check. A-D-13 is **live**: Residue Gate computes the BBS/HAC state chains, encrypts and rewinds Blum–Goldwasser from the transmitted final state, and derives disclosure, reuse, malleability and validity outcomes from the current run while keeping its indexing, prime, toy-cycle and authentication limits explicit. A-D-08 is **live**: Attestation Gate executes a no-application-attribute CDL Pi_DAA+ Setup, Join, BBS+ credential, randomized Sign/Verify and named-basename Link route, then recomputes issuer rotation, re-Join, credential-failure and private-key rogue-list cases while preserving the separate PCR/runtime limitation. Hybrid Sign's TLS assignment-status correction is also live. The counts below describe whole proposals.

E-K-08 is **live** at exact Search Vault main `2ecc6612c260`. Its ninth exhibit runs the existing exact SSE query beside three threshold-specific deletion-gram indexes. Real HMAC-SHA-256 trapdoors, AES-256-GCM candidate payloads, client-side Levenshtein filtering and an independent full-matrix oracle derive the storage/query/response and error ledger; a retained-byte known-anchor control exposes one added neighborhood relation without claiming arbitrary recovery. Exact-main [run 40360215127](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-search-vault/actions/runs/40360215127) passed 209 unit tests, 45 browser/accessibility checks and all 16 clean-baseline mutations, then deployed Pages artifact `12666624873`; all six public files match the artifact and a fresh build byte for byte.

L-R-08 is **live** at exact Search Vault main `28bb6f3c50e0`. Its tenth exhibit separates writer and reader roles, executes pairing-based delegation and authorization failures, and derives the single-server known-query attack from actual iterative-test equality edges. A separate QM/DH route runs fresh query blinding, 128-bit-share zero-sum garbled Bloom filters, packed Paillier selectors, sanitized and shuffled AES-GCM writer envelopes, an independent result oracle and the explicit combined-view collusion failure. Exact-main [run 40369941784](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-search-vault/actions/runs/40369941784) passed 232 unit tests, 51 browser/accessibility checks and both clean-baseline mutation gates with all 16 fuzzy and 17 MUSE mutations caught, then deployed Pages artifact `12673115567`; all nine public files match the artifact and a fresh build byte for byte.

E-K-09 is **live** at exact LLL Break main `3f7ca812858c`. Its sixth exhibit generates and validates a toy Merkle–Hellman key, encrypts and trapdoor-decrypts bits, then gives the original unscaled Lagarias–Odlyzko Algorithm SV basis to the existing LLL reducer using public data only. Candidate rows, public sums and unimodular transforms are independently checked; one visible reproducible failure and the bounded seed sweep are not presented as a density threshold or security result. Exact-main tests and deployment passed, all 19 clean-baseline mutations were killed, and all five public files matched Pages artifact `12622431732` byte for byte.

L-R-02 is **live** at exact MAC Race main `695391521cfd`. The sixth lesson executes byte-aligned Black–Rogaway 2002 PMAC with full tags, official AES-128/AES-256 vectors, Gray-code offsets and distinct complete/partial final rules. It rejects content, position and wrong-final-rule controls and compares exact AES dependency work/span with CMAC without claiming measured parallel speed or Q2 security. Exact-main CI and deployment passed 106 unit and 39 browser/accessibility/cross-browser checks plus all 24 clean-baseline mutation cases; all four public files matched Pages artifact `12621759091` byte for byte.

L-R-06 is **live** at exact Oblivious Shelf main `7529bf0099a8`. Its fifth section executes a bounded 1-private, 2-out-of-5 unit-vector construction over GF(8): five real server dot products form a degree-one Reed–Solomon word, and the decoder derives correction from the received points without fault labels or the retained database bit. Erasure, Byzantine, mixed, stale-replica, custom and exceeded-bound cases are executable; one query view retains every target, while two views interpolate the one-hot vector. Exact-main [run 40349758005](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-oblivious-shelf/actions/runs/40349758005) passed 63 unit tests, eight workflow controls, all 20 hosted browser/accessibility/reflow checks and all 16 clean-baseline mutations, then deployed Pages artifact `12663008444`. Public HTML, JavaScript and CSS matched a fresh exact-main build byte for byte.

E-K-04 and S09 are **live** at exact Hidden Bit main `2f1ea68ada54`. The seventh exhibit retains the adjacent H0–H5, symbolic AES-PRP and bounded hard-core-predicate lesson delivered at `7f143a4e9403`. The eighth exhibit adds separate target-before-key and key-first state machines over an explicit keyed toy family: legal target-first play wins exactly `2/256`, the key-first strategy wins `256/256`, stale or out-of-order moves are refused, and the signed `(key, hash)` pair is kept distinct from a UOWHF, asymptotic-security or real-hash claim. Exact-main CI and deployment passed 148 unit, 51 browser/claim/accessibility and 33 verdict tests; the final-head gate killed all 52 clean-baseline mutations. All 36 public files matched exact-main Pages artifact `12615453283` byte for byte.

RR-SUMCHECK-GKR is also **live** outside the 48-proposal denominator at exact ZK Proof Lab main `df4db000b0c8`. Its eighth exhibit executes sum-check over a validated prime field and a bounded two-layer GKR walkthrough, with challenges sampled only after checked prover messages, direct terminal evaluations, replay and corruption controls, and explicit “not zero knowledge / not a SNARK” limits. Final-head checks passed 94 unit, 63 browser-quality and all 15 clean-baseline mutation controls; exact-main passed 72 hosted browser/accessibility tests and deployed Pages artifact `12615459994`. All 32 public files matched that artifact byte for byte.

L-R-05 and its shared A-D-10 cost-model sub-scope are **live** at exact Shelf Oracle main `d08691c9e17a`. The delivered route is a bounded Piano-style parity-and-refresh lesson for a static public database, not a claim that the existing BFV routes gained sublinear server work or that this small adaptation inherits Piano's full theorem. Its exact-main gate and deployment passed, and the public HTML, JavaScript and CSS matched a fresh build byte for byte.

A-D-03 is **live** at exact Attribute Gate main `a4cd1973f99b`. The delivered lesson uses Figure 5's CP-ABE equations with §3.3's partially hidden CP-A2BE payload, keeps the existing FAME results unchanged, states the hidden-value/public-structure boundary, derives each candidate from real pairing equations, fails closed on malformed or over-bound inputs and labels the AES-GCM candidate-recognition wrapper as a bounded teaching adaptation rather than a transferred theorem. The exact-main gate and deployment passed, and the public HTML, JavaScript and CSS matched a fresh build byte for byte.

L-R-03 is **live** at exact Attribute Gate main `ab293fd0386f`. RW15 and the bounded epoch adaptation remain visibly separate: the former implements the paper's decentralized multi-authority equations and GID binding, while the latter is an owner-assisted application-layer FAME composition with no revocable-ABE, public-update or combined-theorem claim. The exact-main gate and deployment passed, and the public HTML, JavaScript and CSS matched a fresh build byte for byte.

S04 is **live** at exact Proof Tally main `f1220e93baa5`. The delivered lesson keeps standardized Prio3 draft-22 and its known-answer values unchanged, executes fixed-size exact-output differencing, and then releases one bounded noisy sum only when the declared cohort, honest-contributor, non-collusion, complete-delivery and output-cap conditions hold. Exact-main CI and deployment passed, the full 64-mutation replay killed every recorded mutation, and the public HTML, JavaScript and CSS matched a fresh build byte for byte.

S02 is **live** at exact Envelope KMS main `d2b6614a9fad`. The delivered integrity-only teaching adaptation compares a static HMAC key with a domain-separated HMAC-SHA-256 state schedule, executes pre-compromise rewrite, compromised-suffix forgery and truncation cases, and requires distinct external evidence for checkpoint consistency, completeness and freshness. Exact-main CI and deployment passed, all 18 combined claim mutations were killed, and the public HTML, JavaScript and CSS matched the reviewed build byte for byte.

A-D-04 is **live** at exact DC-net main `8de5cb25042c`. The five-participant cycle executes fresh pairwise-mask cancellation, observer and coalition components with prior-preserving posteriors, parity-only collisions, malformed and correctly shaped disruption, dropout and reformation, bounded SHA-256 commit/reveal, complete incident-mask reuse and later disclosure. Exact-main CI and deployment passed 44 unit, 11 learner-claim and 12 accessibility/reflow tests, all 22 clean-baseline mutations were caught, CodeQL passed, and the public HTML, JavaScript and CSS matched the exact merged build byte for byte.

S01 is **live** at exact HPKE Envelope main `fcf3b56c06e1`. The executable comparison derives a fresh Auth ciphertext that the intended recipient accepts as the sender using recipient `skR` and public sender `pkS`, then runs a separate Base-HPKE + strict-Ed25519 Sign-then-Encrypt profile over signed sender, recipient, independently expected context and message fields. Recipient, sender and combined compromise, forwarding, public evidence, replay and tamper cases retain the profile's non-standardization and narrow-evidence boundaries. Exact-main CI passed 258 unit tests, all 19 clean-baseline mutations and 27 hosted browser/accessibility tests, deployed, and the public HTML, JavaScript and CSS matched the exact merged tree byte for byte.

A-D-05 is **live** at exact LFSR Forge main `7c6246133b9e`. The four-input audit computes exact ANF, Walsh, restriction and complete degree-at-most-two annihilator results, then executes three deterministic counterexamples with public-only worker inputs and separate hidden-state checks. Final-head and exact-main gates passed 32 unit tests, all 17 clean-baseline mutations and all 17 hosted browser/accessibility tests; exact main deployed, and the public HTML, JavaScript, CSS and three workers matched the reviewed build byte for byte.

A-D-11 is **live** at exact LFSR Forge main `66d95370b0a3`. The separate three-secret/four-public degree-four toy oracle executes complete cube-attack preprocessing and online phases: 128 preprocessing evaluations recover four exact superpolys, three affine maxterms filter 8 → 4 → 2 → 1, four disjoint held-out rows precede controller comparison, and missing/corrupted-equation cases stop ambiguously or fail verification. Final-head and exact-main gates passed 38 unit tests, all 23 clean-baseline mutations and all 19 hosted browser/accessibility tests; exact main deployed, and the public HTML, JavaScript, CSS and three workers matched the reviewed build byte for byte. The lesson executes no Trivium state or round and makes no reduced-round or full-Trivium attack claim.

A-D-12 is **live** at exact LFSR Forge main `c4ef2da4b33f`. The ninth panel reproduces the reverse-engineered historical A5/1 setup checkpoints and consecutive BGW bursts, exposes majority-clock decisions against a regular-clock counterfactual, and completes a separately bounded 4,096-state exercise with ambiguity, wrong-clock, corruption and held-out checks. Final-head and exact-main gates passed 47 unit tests, all 33 clean-baseline mutations and all 23 hosted browser/accessibility tests; exact main deployed, and all seven public assets matched the reviewed build byte for byte.

L-R-01 is **live** at current exact Clock Arithmetic main `b97b515033e2`. The seventh act derives Montgomery setup, executes REDC with its low radix word, cancellation, radix shift and optional final subtraction, chains encoded products and squares, and measures one fixed 4,096-operation ordinary/Montgomery schedule without treating browser timing as a benchmark or constant-time evidence. An independent post-handoff audit found no arithmetic defect but did find that the root theme pin used an equivalent DOM property outside the Hub's canonical source pattern; [PR #4](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-clock-arithmetic/pull/4) corrected that one-line integration issue. Exact-current-main [run 40240784786](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-clock-arithmetic/actions/runs/40240784786) passed 59 unit tests, all 38 clean-baseline mutations and all 40 hosted browser/accessibility tests; exact main deployed, and all four public assets matched both its Pages artifact and a fresh build byte for byte.

E-K-03 is **live** at implementation merge `6644abcb691b`. The bounded lesson executes ordered SPD policy, four-message PSK IKEv2, two directional Child SAs, exact IPv4 ESP processing, authentication-before-replay-window update, transport fallback/deletion and fresh rekey state. Implementation-main quality/deployment run 40226926121 passed 102 unit tests, seven hosted learner-claim/accessibility/reflow tests and all 17 clean-baseline mutations; CodeQL run 40226926164 passed. Current source-reviewed main `3019cef4c514` adds only a CodeQL workflow dependency update and the one-line dark-theme root pin from PR #4; README and `src/` remain byte-identical to the implementation. Exact-current-main run 40229891529 passed the same core and rendered gates and deployed successfully, CodeQL run 40229891505 passed, and all three public files matched a fresh current-main build byte for byte on 2026-10-25.

A-D-14 is **live** at exact Matsui Line main `7a85fbf1e6f`. The fixed three-round Heys toy joins a chosen `0x0B` differential prefix to a parity suffix at the explicit round-2 input, distinguishes the 9/64 naive heuristic from the 9/32 signed-path estimate and the real 1/4-or-5/16 codebook bias, ranks observable whitening material, verifies a winner on unused pairs and includes a control whose naive 1/16 signal cancels to exact zero. [Exact-main run 40232553089](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-matsui-line/actions/runs/40232553089) passed 122 unit tests, all 32 hosted learner-claim/accessibility/reflow tests and all 15 clean-baseline mutations, deployed, and all five public files matched a fresh exact-main build byte for byte.

A-D-09 is **live** at exact Signature Gate main `db6695e0f136`. The fifth chapter instantiates Jakobsson, Sako and Impagliazzo §5 on the lab's existing confirmation relation: the selected signer or confirmer witness and the registered verifier secret produce proofs accepted by one verifier, an explicit bijection makes the two true-statement transcript distributions identical, and the verifier-secret route also constructs an accepted false-statement transcript. The separate public Fiat–Shamir comparison establishes only publicly recomputable equations here, not witness knowledge, soundness or unforgeability. Exact-main [research/deployment run 40243267066](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-confirmer-signatures/actions/runs/40243267066), [ordinary-signature run 40243267074](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-confirmer-signatures/actions/runs/40243267074) and [real-pairing run 40243267458](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-confirmer-signatures/actions/runs/40243267458) passed 138 root tests, 55 real-pairing tests, all 8 clean-baseline designated-verifier mutations, 15 independent Python checks, 8 symbolic identities, 100,972 exhaustive extractions, 6 browser flows, the Lean build and axiom gate, production build and deployment. All 12 public files matched exact-main Pages artifact `12617637496` byte for byte.

The 48 proposals retain the audit’s stable IDs and priorities: **17 P1 — next, 24 P2 — depth, and 7 P3 — specialist**. Maintenance corrections are separate from that denominator. A proposal can share an implementation with related IDs, but each ID keeps its own acceptance criteria. These are not 48 requests for standalone labs.

This is the implementation ledger for the user-authorized encyclopedia rollout. [concept-coverage.md](../concept-coverage.md) remains the canonical fleet coverage and gap inventory; this document records the accepted scope, next actions and delivery evidence. It does not replace [TASKS.md](../TASKS.md) or the unrelated queue in [futureworkoct.md](../futureworkoct.md).

### First milestone checklist

The final runner was verified at local `2e8efe0fb9d6a07f2c6ce45d658da44fe4f28a1e`; hosted checks gate integration separately.

- [x] Reconcile A-D-06 against current AES Anatomy, verify its primary-source fault model, recovery and negative/countermeasure cases, complete hosted gate, deployment revision and exact served assets, and record it as live rather than duplicating the work.

- [x] Implement A-D-01 in Dedup Vault; independently audit its cryptographic constructions and boundaries, pass its full hosted unit, learner-claim, accessibility and mutation gate, and verify exact-main deployment and served assets.

## Delivery evidence for completed milestones

### Search Vault — E-K-08 fuzzy encrypted search and similarity leakage

- **Merged source:** [Search Vault PR #27](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-search-vault/pull/27), reviewed feature head `f0c1dc41a672b424b65b6ca7cce1b72cb32625ea`, squash-merged as exact main [`2ecc6612c2602c0f01ac9869fb07c589c5c9bc91`](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-search-vault/tree/2ecc6612c2602c0f01ac9869fb07c589c5c9bc91); the two trees are identical.
- **Primary-source and model audit:** The deletion-set and trace model was checked against Jin Li et al., ePrint 2009/593 revision 2009-12-04, §§3.4–6 and Algorithm 2. This implementation uses an exact HMAC/AES-GCM map rather than the paper's trie and performance experiment, and withholds any transferred theorem or general EFSE leakage claim.
- **Independent controls:** Thresholds 0, 1 and 2 are separate alternatives rather than cumulative storage. A rolling-row client distance and full-matrix oracle must agree; the default one-edit typo recovers `audit`, while threshold 2 also returns `lawsuit` as a gram collision at true distance 3 and filters it before document access. Every bounded true neighbor must reach the candidate set, final documents must equal the corpus oracle, invalid input reaches no comparison server, edits retire evidence, ciphertext relocation fails closed, and exact known-anchor equality cannot be mislabeled added fuzzy leakage.
- **Verification:** PR [run 40359240656](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-search-vault/actions/runs/40359240656) and exact-main [run 40360215127](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-search-vault/actions/runs/40360215127) each passed 209 unit tests, a clean unmodified mutation baseline, all 16 named fuzzy-search mutations, the production build and all 45 browser/accessibility checks. Exact-main Pages artifact `12666624873` uploaded at 85,219 bytes with SHA-256 `c0ed0d87ce3ff792d54a9e5512b3586799e57bb89237a356849ab78bfeaa25e3` and deployed successfully. All six artifact files matched a fresh exact-main build and the public site byte for byte: HTML `fbf178d254fe7a8dd06cff76e4fc48ccb0eb04ecfb0da77ebca87f3e099d5bcb`, entry JavaScript `56f25b59488e18ee30111356a4707660904b2dbfbfaeaeb676a40c0705a53fce`, CSS `bf4780369cc5a73dca5f9f7bd1841a686be79d21279b97a48578f13bec702fde`, core JavaScript `e27ae7c2c5177908725adfe31961a0b26ed5e5da88bdfd55e8a8ea4401dafecb`, dictionary JavaScript `640d7c1142d8f27425196ece20f006d8911d0ef4618ae3ec8d09a5c58ad46c20` and server JavaScript `61fbeac55cb04ea6fd62a8701b175da6e895de83d68a786f3761f9c3ca47be6f`.
- **Retained limits:** Fixed 14-keyword, 24-document ASCII corpus and thresholds 0–2 only; no symbol trie, LSH, ranking, semantic or transposition search, dynamic or multi-user index, malicious-server result proof, network/process separation, deployment benchmark or production hardening. Outside context supplies three prior query labels; a bounded non-link is not a privacy proof.

### Search Vault — L-R-08 multi-user search and collusion

- **Merged source:** [Search Vault PR #28](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-search-vault/pull/28), reviewed feature head `1f54229461b80f9fbc6e59cbaa9922edb626cc13`, squash-merged as exact main [`28bb6f3c50e0584b5b94e0a5a0c0b9f2ef52141d`](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-search-vault/tree/28bb6f3c50e0584b5b94e0a5a0c0b9f2ef52141d); the two trees are identical.
- **Primary-source and model audit:** The single-server construction and label propagation were checked against Van Rompay, Molva and Önen, PoPETs 2017, §§3–4 and Figure 2. The split was checked against their SCC 2018 paper, §§2–7 and Appendices A–C. The browser uses a scoped Type-3 BLS12-381 and packed-Paillier adaptation and does not claim the complete optimized protocol or transferred proof.
- **Independent controls:** Role and authorization failures are atomic. The attack receives server equality edges and one declared label, not plaintext keywords or oracle truth. A changed keyword remains outside the known equivalence class. The split output is checked against a separate plaintext oracle, false positives and negatives are reported, repeated queries require fresh trapdoor and selector bytes, and combining QM positivity with DH routing deliberately demonstrates the non-collusion failure boundary.
- **Verification:** Initial PR run `40368568981` failed only the new end-to-end test at Vitest's five-second default while completing in 5.5 seconds; the corrected per-test 20-second bound retained the workload and assertions. PR [run 40368903846](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-search-vault/actions/runs/40368903846) and exact-main [run 40369941784](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-search-vault/actions/runs/40369941784) then passed 232 unit tests, clean fuzzy and MUSE mutation baselines, all 16 and 17 named mutations, production build and all 51 browser/accessibility checks with no retries. Pages artifact `12673115567` (95,794 bytes; ZIP SHA-256 `c8b32e9259ab822f34e6520e47f29e7c9f022c5c5d69f37b2e51038a5c961699`) deployed through deployment `7339158509`; all nine artifact files matched a fresh exact-main build and the public site byte for byte.
- **Retained limits:** Three writers, three readers, static exact keywords, public authorization and a non-production 1024-bit Paillier modulus; no recursive OT, in-line ZGBF, optimized parameters, malicious security, revocation, dynamic or conjunctive search, process isolation, complete-paper reproduction or privacy after QM/DH collusion.

### Oblivious Shelf — L-R-06 robust PIR

- **Merged source:** [Oblivious Shelf PR #24](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-oblivious-shelf/pull/24), reviewed feature head `23fe2fe934f1600afbf6b7e3b70510437ab45479`, squash-merged as exact main [`7529bf0099a8fb835a7108150ca082e61928ba7e`](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-oblivious-shelf/tree/7529bf0099a8fb835a7108150ca082e61928ba7e); the two trees are identical.
- **Primary-source audit:** The bounded lesson was checked against Beimel and Stahl, §§2.2, 2.3.1 and 5–6; Goldberg 2007, §§1–3; and Shamir 1979. It deliberately implements a smaller 1-private, 2-out-of-5 unit-vector construction over GF(8), not Beimel–Stahl Theorem 5.2 or Goldberg's full robust protocol.
- **Independent controls and corrections:** Five actual server dot products form a degree-one Reed–Solomon word. The decoder derives results from received points without fault labels or the retained database bit; it corrects exactly the modeled patterns satisfying `2b+u <= 3`, and a pinned beyond-bound transcript accepts the opposite bit rather than being mislabeled detected. One query view remains compatible with every target, while two views interpolate the one-hot vector. Hosted checks also found and fixed an inaccessible generic labelled container, a pre-existing non-keyboard-scrollable mobile table and a mutation filter whose unescaped test-name regex could select zero tests.
- **Verification:** Exact-main [run 40349758005](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-oblivious-shelf/actions/runs/40349758005) passed 63 unit tests, eight workflow controls, all 20 hosted browser/accessibility/reflow checks and all 16 clean-baseline mutations, then deployed Pages artifact `12663008444`. Public HTML, JavaScript and CSS matched a fresh exact-main build byte for byte.
- **Retained limits:** This route neither authenticates answers nor proves freshness, and privacy assumes at most one query recipient. It is not Percy++, RAID-PIR, a sublinear-communication construction, a deployment protocol or a hardened implementation.

### Shamir Gate — S07 visual secret sharing

- **Merged source:** [Shamir Gate PR #18](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-shamir-gate/pull/18), reviewed feature head `ba8832470181430923df0e565e2d1bc6e1c272a4`, squash-merged as exact main [`48a7a3ab9298edf708f5496159c39f2cdb9d3227`](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-shamir-gate/tree/48a7a3ab9298edf708f5496159c39f2cdb9d3227).
- **Primary-source audit:** The construction was checked against Naor and Shamir's EUROCRYPT 1994 paper, §§2–3. The implementation uses the paper's Boolean-OR observation model and exact full restricted-matrix distribution definition, then instantiates the 2-of-2 same/complement construction with four subpixels arranged as a 2-by-2 block. The retrieved Springer PDF had SHA-256 `ce30fcd83398286fb72e6200b507d276a2d597a2614b0dffe0176897e92681f3`.
- **Independent controls and corrections:** Complete one-share block distributions are enumerated rather than inferred from equal black-cell counts. Physical OR, digital XOR, polynomial Shamir and position-only scrambling remain distinct. Mask reuse exposes relations, and missing-share reconstruction refuses to run. Hosted browser checks additionally found and fixed keyboard access for scrollable tables, an intrinsic-grid overflow in the existing Gate tab and long metadata overflow at 320 pixels; the reflow assertion now names the exact offending element.
- **Verification:** Exact-main [run 40329795463](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-shamir-gate/actions/runs/40329795463) passed 73 unit tests, two publication controls, all 14 clean-baseline mutations, the production build and all 19 hosted browser/accessibility/reflow checks. Pages artifact `12654776334` deployed through deployment `7332922632`; public HTML SHA-256 `16cb595e…`, CSS `d5e6efd1…` and JavaScript `633f813c…` matched the artifact byte for byte.
- **Retained limits:** Binary 2-of-2 only; exact alignment and fresh independent per-pixel masks are assumed. There is no authenticity, k-of-n extension, grayscale/color/progressive construction, printing or optical-error model, operational custody, browser-state isolation or production-hardening claim.

### Distance Bounding — S06 Hancke–Kuhn proximity lesson

- **Merged source:** [Distance Bounding PR #1](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-distance-bounding/pull/1), independently corrected at reviewed feature head `43b6c3d11094c44bf09f33437295d0720f7bc16c`, squash-merged as `5f0b6a208fd240af7560b51bccd7760e121d3891`; the two trees are identical. [PR #3](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-distance-bounding/pull/3) corrected only the README's mutation count at reviewed head `28572e70b05ca29be538324f57784357edfbb95d`, squash-merged as current exact main [`4458307b57a0`](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-distance-bounding/tree/4458307b57a02da4362cee5b799f20bdade03fd4); those trees are also identical.
- **Primary-source and model audit:** The protocol route follows Hancke and Kuhn's original verifier-nonce, one-execution profile. The implementation declares HMAC-SHA-256 as its PRF instantiation, uses exact `BigInt` rational timing and probability arithmetic, checks rapid response content separately from timing, and conditions its bounded distance-fraud figure on the actual response-register distance. The page distinguishes its deterministic event model from physical radio ranging and makes the `(3/4)^n` result conditional on the stated unpredictable-challenge/pre-ask model.
- **Independent corrections:** Audit replaced a vacuous ordinary-authentication self-comparison with a domain-separated HMAC challenge/tag and tamper control, changed simulation sampling to exact rejection sampling, corrected partial-preask/predictable-challenge formulas, restored the dishonest prover's two propagation legs, pinned one dark theme, made empty inputs fail closed and expanded the clean-baseline mutation gate from 11 to 15 cases.
- **Verification:** Exact-main [CodeQL run 40312351878](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-distance-bounding/actions/runs/40312351878) passed. Exact-main quality/deployment [run 40312352129](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-distance-bounding/actions/runs/40312352129) passed format, typecheck, lint, 96 unit tests, coverage of 99.44% statements / 98.42% branches / 100% functions / 100% lines, the production build, dependency audit, 20 learner-claim checks, 10 accessibility/reflow/keyboard checks and a clean baseline followed by all 15 named mutations caught. Pages artifact `12644478979` has ZIP SHA-256 `e4aa99063da7cff5d7e93c54c5425139a174bd4e85cca8dbe7a19a993c47a3c9`; all three public files match the artifact and a fresh exact-main build byte for byte.
- **Retained limits:** This is a bounded protocol/event lesson, not UWB or other radio hardware, a browser wall-clock measurement, a deployment-ready authenticator or a proof against every relay/physical-layer technique. The original paper specifies no PRF instantiation; the chosen HMAC profile and deterministic fixtures are teaching choices, JavaScript secret handling is not constant-time, and the prover-nonce protocol variant is named but not implemented.

### IBE Gate — A-D-07 certificateless ownership and escrow comparison

The reviewed feature and merge trees are identical.
- **Primary-source audit:** The actor split was checked against Al-Riyami–Paterson's Type-I/Type-II model, and the executable `IBE → PKE → one-time MAC` order was checked against Huang–Wong 2007 §§4 and 4.2. The learner page explicitly withholds the latter paper's theorem because the reused BasicIdent is only IND-CPA rather than the theorem's required IND-ID-CCA2 IBE.
- **Independent corrections:** The final route cryptographically binds identity and application context inside the BasicIdent input, authenticates the serialized inner ciphertext through HMAC-SHA-256, gives each adversary API only its declared capabilities, keeps one public reject result separate from the omniscient teaching trace, and compares a real P-256-signed RSA-OAEP PKI route with the existing escrowed IBE route. Displacement denial, tag mutation and combined compromise are executable controls.
- **Gates:** Exact-head [run 40309202902](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-ibe-gate/actions/runs/40309202902) passed dependency audit, four test files with 50 tests, 20 hosted browser/accessibility checks, and a clean baseline followed by all 10 mutations caught and restored. Exact-main/deployment [run 40310063824](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-ibe-gate/actions/runs/40310063824) passed on the merge.
- **Live evidence:** Pages artifact `12645010926`, a fresh exact-main build and [the public lesson](https://systemslibrarian.github.io/crypto-lab-ibe-gate/) had byte-identical HTML (`447f7aed…`), CSS (`d7803b28…`) and JavaScript (`a8406ef0…`).
- **Retained boundary:** The one-shot cases are not full adaptive security games, there are no extraction/decryption oracles, JavaScript is not constant-time, public-key replacement can deny service, and an authenticated directory remains outside the CLE envelope.

- **Merged source:** [Residue Gate PR #1](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-residue-gate/pull/1), reviewed head `a36e190dda24`, squash-merged as exact main [`f20e6db42edc`](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-residue-gate/tree/f20e6db42edc5d654c9934dda27855dd6e340dfa). - **Primary-source audit:** The implementation was checked against Blum, Blum and Shub 1986 §§4 and 7, Blum and Goldwasser 1984's encoding plus Algorithms A/B and decoding lemma, and HAC Algorithms 5.40, 8.55–8.56, Example 8.57 and Notes 8.58–8.59. The page names the one-squaring indexing difference instead of combining incompatible published conventions.
- **Independent corrections:** Successive audits corrected the disclosure boundary, public toy-cycle predecessor, fixed/two-state seed cases, GM/BG assumption split, prime/probable-prime wording, short-final-block adaptation, empty-message evidence, sender/wire separation, stale-response and worker-failure lifecycle, equal-prime rationale, repeated-final-state condition, HAC's four-exponentiation count and the public-only source boundary. No retry or infrastructure-failure path counted as a kill.

Deployment `7323506551` / status `20586036360` succeeded; the public HTML, JavaScript, CSS and worker matched that artifact and a fresh exact-main archive build byte for byte.
- **Retained boundary:** HAC indexing and distinct probable Blum primes are explicit; original BG's stronger prime condition is not silently transferred. The short final block is an adaptation. BG is unauthenticated and malleable, QR validity is not authentication, toy-cycle recovery is not a general backwards-unpredictability result, random-looking output is not a pseudorandomness proof, this is unrelated to BBS credential signatures, and JavaScript retirement is not physical memory zeroization.

### LLL Break — E-K-09 Merkle–Hellman and Algorithm SV

- **Merged source:** Corrected [PR #27](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-lll-break/pull/27), reviewed head `525f9e34e8a04a85ab73a8662e284d63c7728109`, squash-merged as exact main [`3f7ca812858c`](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-lll-break/tree/3f7ca812858c87e0ad6d3c403c86c705dde3e16a). The reviewed feature and merge trees are identical.
- **Independent corrections:** The first candidate falsely said none of seeds 1–80 failed at `k=3`; exact recomputation found two, so the corrected copy pins seven failures at `k=1`, two at `k=3` and none at `k=5`. Its arithmetic screen had also bounded only Algorithm SV's first target even though the complement pass could exceed the safe input range. The corrected branch screens both targets, verifies the exact unimodular transform before reading candidates, adds pass-2-only and endpoint controls, and exposes a reproducible in-budget failure. Source equations were checked on rendered primary-paper pages rather than trusting OCR-corrupted inequalities and primes.
- **Gates:** Final-head [run 40254281402](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-lll-break/actions/runs/40254281402) and exact-main test [run 40254867941](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-lll-break/actions/runs/40254867941) passed 24/24 knapsack checks, two publication controls, the production build, 29 browser checks, two accessibility/reflow checks and all 19 clean-baseline mutations. Exact-main deploy [run 40254867984](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-lll-break/actions/runs/40254867984) succeeded.
- **Live evidence:** Pages artifact `12622431732` deployed; all five public files matched the artifact, exact-main build and served site byte for byte.
- **Retained boundary:** this is the original unscaled Algorithm SV route on selected toy instances. It recovers message bits, not the original or an equivalent trapdoor; it is not Shamir's attack or the Coster embedding, and bounded failures or finite sweeps establish neither a density threshold nor security.

### Signature Gate — A-D-09 noninteractive designated-verifier proof

- **Merged source:** [PR #15](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-confirmer-signatures/pull/15), reviewed head [`1e01c0641be6`](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-confirmer-signatures/tree/1e01c0641be6506fcf470ae02cee51741432003f), squash-merged as exact main [`db6695e0f136`](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-confirmer-signatures/tree/db6695e0f136507572dcc10e652bff83ea357383). The reviewed feature and merge trees are identical.
- **Primary-source audit:** The implementation was checked against Jakobsson, Sako and Impagliazzo, EUROCRYPT '96: §2, Definitions 1–2 in §3, construct/verify/simulate in §5, verifier-secret knowledge in §7 and strong designation in Appendix Definition 3. The chapter implements §5's concrete trapdoor-commitment realization of the semantic disjunction “the statement is true, or I am the verifier.” It does not implement the separate generic Cramer–Damgård–Schoenmakers OR composition suggested by the encyclopedia entry.
- **Independent corrections:** The first handoff's mutation script had not run in hosted CI; the workflow now invokes all eight mutations on pull requests and main. The first corrected hosted run then caught a stale browser assertion at both widths. The audit also removed wording that treated refusal of the verifier secret by the honest public-prover API as a general proof-of-knowledge or unforgeability result. The public comparison now claims only that any reader can recompute its two equations.
- **Gates:** Exact-main [research/deployment run 40243267066](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-confirmer-signatures/actions/runs/40243267066) passed 138 root tests, 55 real-pairing tests, all 8 named clean-baseline mutations, 15 independent `py_ecc` checks, 8 symbolic polynomial identities, 100,972 exhaustive two-challenge extractions, 4 ordinary-browser and 2 real-pairing browser/accessibility/reflow tests, the production build, and a Lean build whose axiom guard checked 14 declarations. [Ordinary-signature run 40243267074](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-confirmer-signatures/actions/runs/40243267074) and [real-pairing run 40243267458](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-confirmer-signatures/actions/runs/40243267458) also passed on the exact merge SHA.
- **Live evidence:** Pages artifact `12617637496`, SHA-256 `47502d0603808660507d5cb069d3f630a2506a95df688c1971616619ad58b325`, names `db6695e0f136507572dcc10e652bff83ea357383` in `revision.json`. All 12 deployed files fetched from [Signature Gate](https://systemslibrarian.github.io/crypto-lab-confirmer-signatures/) matched that artifact byte for byte.
- **Retained limits:** This is Definition 1 only, not a strong designated verifier, designated-verifier signature or complete designated-confirmer-signature construction. The Type-3 statement and expanded hash binding are explicit teaching adaptations. The paper supplies no §5 security theorem, and this lab does not add one; its exact distribution result is algebra about the two true-statement transcript generators, not a security proof of the surrounding DCS.

### Matsui Line — A-D-14 differential–linear composition

- **Merged source:** [PR #26](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-matsui-line/pull/26), exact main [`7a85fbf1e6f`](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-matsui-line/tree/7a85fbf1e6f566f7b0b718cb93f5dd737950faaa); reviewed feature `c66c4fda832f915d63a93d661d3a05b1436afaef` and the squash merge have identical trees.
- **Gates:** final-head [run 40231832128](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-matsui-line/actions/runs/40231832128) passed. Exact-main quality/deployment [run 40232553089](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-matsui-line/actions/runs/40232553089) repeated 122 unit tests, the production build, 30 learner-behavior plus two axe accessibility/reflow tests and all 15 named mutations. Every mutation began from a passing baseline, changed source and the built bundle, failed its owning assertion, restored exactly and returned green; the summary reports `15 of 15 killed; zero retries and skips`. Mutation artifact `12614330289` is 9,729 bytes. Build job `121287177310` and deploy job `121289575982` succeeded; Pages artifact `12614200540` is 47,730 bytes and identifies exact main.
- **Delivered lesson:** one fixed three-round 8-bit Heys toy joins chosen pairs with difference `0x0B` to a parity suffix at the state entering round 2. The page computes the DDT row, the 9/64 p-weighted heuristic, the 9/32 signed-path estimate and the current key's real 1/4-or-5/16 codebook bias. It ranks 256 observable final-whitening-byte hypotheses and independently checks the winner on unused pairs when the budget leaves a holdout. The `lambda-in = 0x04` control exposes a naive 1/16 estimate but zero signed-path and full-codebook bias and honestly ranks only 16 visible high-nibble hypotheses. The deterministic 512-key odd-stride curve is 31.3% / 66.8% / 90.2% / 99.8% / 100% at 16 / 32 / 64 / 96 / 128 pairs, with ties counted only as partial recovery.
- **Live evidence:** all five public files matched a fresh exact-main build byte for byte on 2026-10-25: `index.html` (38,920 bytes, SHA-256 `34c484f6fc436dd50e5fee498faf456af21d55811e9a19d0e915c50bf185889e`), differential–linear worker (4,766 bytes, `095571848fe1c6db575de91af193d2735930af53c63ae65483348e28d28b5512`), measurement worker (5,117 bytes, `6b368dab13aa44e0a25a1cc101eaae2a1230a4ef9f1e8acb3d414ec273fbd574`), CSS (26,898 bytes, `dcf15b92f3e3c0bf2874a70759aae136ad62acb55a27751929cbd18d2524b8cb`) and entry JavaScript (80,582 bytes, `98be069b953b3fcc1f72d376a596338b11a820dd57fa6e75e4b9d92d9259f1e0`). The public HTML contains the ninth exhibit, “Weld the two attacks together.”
- **Hub publication:** [Crypto Lab PR #295](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab/pull/295) squash-merged as exact Hub main [`e33d4fcb2f3e`](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab/tree/e33d4fcb2f3e3747df7adc81971432c1196142c6); reviewed feature `42a82a378fa2bb0fec76ed107fabb77831c2294f` and the merge have identical trees. Six PR workflows passed 11 applicable jobs and seven exact-main workflows passed 14 jobs, with only the event-inapplicable drift job skipped in each event: [public evidence](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab/actions/runs/40234696706), [README/catalog](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab/actions/runs/40234696714), [arcade games](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab/actions/runs/40234696745), [learning games](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab/actions/runs/40234696696), [protocol workshops](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab/actions/runs/40234696709), [Teach pages](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab/actions/runs/40234696715) and [Pages](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab/actions/runs/40234696375). Pages artifact `12614402618` is 5,780,522 bytes. All six public Hub files matched exact main byte for byte; `index.html` is 827,028 bytes at SHA-256 `84734826ba183f5f50b9ee94e330476d3469e83a83fda44f03bd37cf6dd7b8ad`, and this roadmap before the present evidence-only follow-up is 335,478 bytes at `6d592630c83fb88ac227a51c7f51d444939c12d6f46e0b1760151ce96e3ef0fd`.
- **Retained boundary:** this is a bounded fixed-mask lesson, not evidence that the masks are optimal. It does not recover the 16-bit master key, attack the full four-round toy cipher, reproduce DES, attack production cryptography or treat a deterministic 512-key sample as a random-population estimate.

### Hidden Bit — S09 target-collision order games

- **Merged source:** [PR #17](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-hidden-bit/pull/17), exact main [`2f1ea68ada54`](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-hidden-bit/tree/2f1ea68ada54b88a89ccb0ae2c026eccabd37a83); reviewed feature `2b899037ac7e987f2b9db672039328cb5cc36f78` and the squash merge have identical trees.
- **Gates:** final-head [run 40236124940](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-hidden-bit/actions/runs/40236124940) and exact-main [run 40237685921](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-hidden-bit/actions/runs/40237685921) passed. Exact main reports 148 unit tests, the production build, all 51 hosted browser/claim/accessibility tests and all 33 verdict tests. The final-head mutation gate started each case from a passing baseline and killed all 52 named mutations.
- **Delivered lesson:** separate controllers enforce target-before-key and key-first order over the 8-bit masked family `H_s(x)=h(x xor s)`. In the planted-pair route, `h` is the identity except for the published inner collision `{0x0F, 0xF0}`. Exhaustive enumeration yields exact success counts `2/256` and `256/256`; shifting that planted pair back through the mask is legal only after seeing `s`. Setup changes retire prior results, illegal repeats and stale actions fail closed, and the signed `(key, digest)` output is computed from the current run.
- **Live evidence:** build job `121302656759`, verdict-coverage job `121302656591` and deploy job `121306432401` succeeded. Pages artifact `12615453283` has ZIP SHA-256 `1b43693ac97080b69544814557c16cdc00c6892a50121578035cd28068702a78`; all 36 served files matched it byte for byte. The artifact manifest digest is `8efbbef356ba591e3c7ab91dc7e938af0e2b5a9dbd37c505db6bf5bea2168abe`.
- **Retained boundary:** this exact finite family separates game order; it does not establish a UOWHF construction, asymptotic target-collision resistance or an attack on AES or a real hash function.

- **Delivered lesson:** Act A enumerates an explicit Boolean-hypercube sum, checks every univariate degree and recurrence before sampling the next challenge, and ends in the verifier's direct evaluation. Act B reuses that verifier for one fixed two-layer multilinear circuit reduction and terminates in the verifier's own public-input-layer evaluation. Wrong-sum, carried-lie, excessive-degree, final-evaluation, malformed-replay, challenge-order, tiny-field and four GKR corruption controls are derived from protocol state.
- **Live evidence:** browser-quality job `121304665493`, build job `121304665688` and deploy job `121306158054` succeeded. - **Retained boundary:** the exhibit is an interactive-proof/verifiable-computation teaching slice for one explicit sum-check and one bounded two-layer GKR reduction. It is not zero knowledge, a SNARK, a proof of general GKR soundness or a production prover/verifier.

The state-completion route assumes extraordinary exact state disclosure and separately compares held-out keystream and controller state; it is not a published A5/1 attack or Kc recovery.

- **Retained boundary:** theoretical Walsh and restriction probabilities require independent uniform input bits; deterministic LFSR agreement is measured separately. Exactly four inputs make the degree-two annihilator search complete, but high algebraic immunity does not exclude every fast algebraic relation. The 127-state and 105-pair scans are bounded exhaustive teaching cases, not production fast-correlation attacks or general security evidence.

The served bundle contains the executable Auth KCI case, bounded strict-Ed25519 Sign-then-Encrypt construction, nine-case compromise/evidence matrix, independently pinned-key warning, replay result and current standards-status limitation.
- **Retained boundary:** the profile is not a standardized signcryption wire format, production protocol or proof transfer. Public signature evidence covers exact disclosed fields under a separately pinned key; it does not prove ciphertext authorship, transmission, receipt, time, human intent or legal non-repudiation. Replay and identity-to-key distribution remain application responsibilities.

The delivered exercise is a bounded DupLESS-shaped adaptation, not byte-for-byte DupLESS and not a transfer of the original RSA-OPRF proof to RFC 9497 VOPRF. Each derived AES-CTR key/counter pair is confined to one distinct plaintext value; repeats of that exact value intentionally reuse the pair and ciphertext, while using the pair across different plaintext values would be unsafe. Whole-ciphertext possession is deliberately not presented as a scalable proof-of-ownership protocol, rate limiting only delays guesses within the modeled identities and epochs, and the co-located browser roles do not supply production isolation, authentication, HSM protection or retroactive revocation.

Exact XSet membership removes probabilistic-filter false positives only for honest execution. A malicious server can omit a valid match or falsely include an authentic s-list row that failed the x test, and the adapter has no completeness or freshness proof. The Path ORAM table is derived arithmetic for a separate non-colluding semi-honest storage server, not an executed ORAM or concealment of OXT search leakage. The delivered lesson is a bounded query-compression adaptation, not SealPIR's multidimensional production design. Its four fixed blocks and four fixed responses prevent the chosen 16-record block from being sent in plaintext, but still scan all 64 records and do not establish sublinear server work. Key-dependent Galois keys add the displayed circular-security assumption; the Homomorphic Encryption Standard comparison is limited to its modulus-bit ceiling because the lab uses a different error distribution; and the record tag does not provide malicious-server integrity.

Setup streams 32,768 bytes, performs 2,496 parity-record contributions and retains a 197,696-byte base encoding before sparse cache entries. |
| Online recovery and refresh | Each canonical request serializes to exactly 8 bytes, names one record per chunk, causes exactly eight record probes/XOR contributions and receives one 512-byte parity. Recovery and consumed-backup refresh are checked across all 512 bytes; `replacement = target` and duplicate replacements remain valid rather than being resampled away. |
| Stateful and negative cases | All 64 targets, cached-repeat cover queries, setup misses, the 33-request boundary, malformed and abandoned responses, state loss, canonical persistence, external high-water rollback rejection, a pending-request update and full database-update invalidation/rebuild have unit coverage. The visible update diagnostic discloses that it deliberately chooses a retained replacement from private audit state and is not an ordinary privacy experiment. |
| Availability and costs | The page derives `(7/8)^256` for a fixed target and the scoped session union bound `33 · (7/8)^249 ≈ 1.20 × 10^-13 ≈ 2^-42.92`; neither is presented as a 128-bit or end-to-end bound. Setup, retained state, wire bytes, probes, XOR contributions, cache growth, update scans and rebuilds remain separate. The 33-query normal-cache ceiling is 16,929 bytes, and the full-download wire crossover occurs at query two under the displayed comparison. |
| Browser and accessibility | All 38 hosted Chromium tests passed, including four stateful learner-claim tests and three complete WCAG A/AA/reflow drives at 1,280, 380 and 280 pixels. |

The parser probes are recorded separately and are not added to the runner's seventeen-mutation total. |

This is a bounded adaptation of Piano's primary/backup-hint idea, not its full theorem, reference wire format, benchmark, pipelined construction, dynamic extension or asymptotic storage regime. The server retains only the ordinary database, so `ProcessDB` is identity/none; the expensive setup is client-specific and the explicit retained state is larger than this toy database. The model is static and honest-but-curious. It does not provide malicious-server privacy or integrity, authenticated freshness, availability, timing or traffic hiding, multi-client sharing, hardened state storage, crash consistency, incremental updates or ORAM. Record hashes and the snapshot digest are diagnostic, not authentication, and logical operation counts are not browser timings.

- **Merged source:** [PR #13](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-attribute-gate/pull/13), commit [`ab293fd0386f`](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-attribute-gate/tree/ab293fd0386fac2a4e89cede2b414056df3188eb).

- **Observed live artifact, 2026-10-24 17:39 UTC:** the public page names and renders the RW15 multi-authority and bounded epoch-gating lessons. Public HTML, JavaScript and CSS matched a fresh production build of the merged tree byte for byte; the served bundle retains the RW15 setup/success controls, epoch-adaptation boundary, rollback verdict and payload-preserving rewrap verdict.

| Verification | Observed result |
| --- | --- |
| RW15 construction | Two independent authority setups execute the asymmetric RW15 Type-3 equations. Authority-qualified attributes issued to one canonical byte-exact GID open the two-authority policy; the strongest raw cross-GID row pool structurally satisfies the LSSS but recovers the wrong `GT` element and fails AES-GCM authentication. A compromised Hospital authority can mint Hospital attributes for any GID and open a Hospital-only branch, but cannot manufacture Board's component. |
| Equation and primitive checks | Tests independently reconstruct both authority public keys, issued components, share vectors, every ciphertext row and the three-pairing row identity. Five RFC 9380 §J.10.1 BLS12-381 G2 vectors pin the primitive used for `H(GID)` and `F(attribute)`, with separate scheme-specific domain separation tags. |
| Epoch and retained-access model | The separate application-layer FAME adaptation issues a fresh complete business-plus-epoch key, withholds it from a revoked holder and demonstrates that an old key plus old header still opens. A separately trusted checkpoint binds object, epoch, revision and the complete access-header digest; rolling storage and checkpoint back together remains outside the detector. |
| Rewrap, escrow and work | A fresh DEK encrypts the payload once. Owner-assisted rewrap recovers that DEK through the owner capsule and changes only the selected access header; the UI verifies byte-identical payload ciphertext, exposes owner and FAME-authority escrow, and reports counters incremented by the executed lifecycle rather than a hardcoded estimate. |
| Negative and mutation verification | Malformed group/runtime records, tampering, cross-record payload/header splicing and incorrect metadata fail closed before or at the named authentication boundary. Three L-R-03-specific source mutations—constant GID hashing, `AND`→`OR` epoch gating and unconditional checkpoint acceptance—kept the build green and were killed by two, three and three focused failures respectively; source and bundle hashes returned exactly after every restoration. |
| Served HTML | `index.html`, 8,768 bytes; SHA-256 `406e839b3a7b42b7be4b01d9335574d5da4b30d213438a3d83c3c95e1397bc32`. |
| Served JavaScript | `assets/index-D9lzyOmQ.js`, 229,770 bytes; SHA-256 `a3d6c05f02971c33afd734b5b5bf34ad869c39b0bebe7a3d62ace361fa253838`. |
| Served CSS | `assets/index-D6XbMU7M.css`, 14,946 bytes; SHA-256 `9ad65c1102c7c3b8fd1619df58d75fe7527dd1474880140f9ca99eaf0afdd842`. |

RW15's cited theorem is static random-oracle `q`-DPBDHE2 security with statically declared corrupt authorities under its challenge restriction. The page tests a narrower exposed-authority-secret case and does not claim rogue-key, adaptive-corruption, directory, CCA, policy-privacy, sender-authentication or production security. The epoch route is not RW15, revocable ABE, revocable-storage ABE or self-updatable encryption: it assumes authenticated issuer state and key delivery, performs owner-assisted per-record rewrap, cannot erase retained data and supplies no public update token or combined theorem.

### Hub catalog and roadmap

The encyclopedia integration snapshot documented in this subsection recorded **244 labs** and **1,749 reviewed sections**, twenty more than the 1,729 on that snapshot's prior Hub baseline. In addition to preserving the existing LFSR Trivium, first Envelope KMS key-handle and Search Vault query-integrity sections, it maps Envelope KMS's bounded key-handle state search and S02 key-evolving audit-log comparison, IBE Gate's executable HIBE delegation, AES Anatomy's classical DFA and accepted-output fault lessons, Dedup Vault's five A-D-01 exhibits, Search Vault's executable A-D-02 PEKS exhibit and bounded L-R-07 OXT exhibit, Shelf Oracle's eleven re-anchored PIR exhibits, Attribute Gate's ninth through eleventh separately scoped FABESA, RW15 and epoch-gating exhibits, and Proof Tally's exact-release differencing, participant-contributed noisy release and trust-boundary comparison. The Envelope exhibits keep the basic unkeyed hash chain, key-handle policy model and separately bounded HMAC-SHA-256 compromise lesson distinct; the S02 map does not collapse retained-prefix consistency into completeness or freshness. The Shelf exhibit records its bounded stateful client-preprocessing route separately from the direct and compressed full-scan BFV routes; the Attribute Gate exhibits keep partial policy hiding, independent authority/GID binding and the application-layer epoch adaptation distinct from the unchanged public-policy FAME route and from one another; the Proof Tally exhibits keep the deliberately insecure exact-release control, the executable distributed route and the central/local/shuffle comparison distinct. The integration also retains Hybrid Sign's exact source and exhibit pins after its TLS assignment-status correction. It updates the affected card and knowledge map, pins the reviewed source revisions for that delivery, and retains Search Vault's repaired query-integrity catalog prose. The forty concept boundaries and their statuses remain unchanged.

The source mappings in that snapshot were checked against committed AES Anatomy `1f0f6b86a2dd`, IBE Gate `7dfebaff27c4`, Dedup Vault `3cbf467bdb1a`, Hybrid Sign `e9e4ddb545c6`, Search Vault `d904972690e1`, Shelf Oracle `d08691c9e17a`, Attribute Gate `ab293fd0386f` and Envelope KMS `d2b6614a9fad`; LFSR Forge was separately re-reconciled against its then-current main and live deployment. The scoped algorithm review keeps excluded side-channel and mutation-only fault mentions out of implementation credit, distinguishes differential fault analysis from ordinary differential cryptanalysis and deterministic finite automata, and records SIFA, HIBE, the S02 private-verifier log, the DupLESS-shaped VOPRF substitution, Type-3 PEKS, bounded OXT, Shelf Oracle's bounded four-block expansion, its bounded Piano-style stateful route, Attribute Gate's bounded FABESA candidate-opening wrapper and its application-layer epoch gate as teaching adaptations rather than inheriting their papers' full results. RW15 alone is mapped to its executed paper equations; the separate rewrap route is not catalogued as a revocable-ABE construction. Shelf Oracle's IND-CPA prose, Search Vault sibling link and side-channel limitation remain explicit non-attack evidence; prose matches for DES and the Gaussian mechanism remain excluded references. Hybrid Sign's modeled break scenarios are not credited as executed discrete-log, factoring or side-channel attacks, and ordinary-language pairing is not treated as a bilinear-pairing reference. The `ed25519.js` module filename used to import noble's ristretto255 group is not catalogued as an Ed25519 implementation or reference, and Attribute Gate's `bls12_381` group import is not catalogued as a BLS signature implementation. Catalog schema/source checks, generated-file freshness checks, search/structure checks, knowledge-map assertions, teaching-scope controls and the broader non-browser CI suites gate this integration. This is verification of the changed mappings, not a new runtime test of every lab in that 244-lab snapshot.

The S02, A-D-01, A-D-02, L-R-07, L-R-04, L-R-05 and A-D-10 integrations close vocabulary gaps rather than leaving their central claims as unchecked card labels. Envelope KMS now has an exact HMAC implementation anchor and a separately reviewed Merkle-tree comparison without invented fault or side-channel attack credit. Message-locked encryption has a reviewed implementation anchor in Dedup Vault; PEKS and OXT have exact Search Vault source anchors with their searchable-encryption family and non-standardization boundaries recorded; and Shelf Oracle's BFV, NTT, Ring-LWE and PIR credits point to executable source while its eleven exhibits distinguish direct selection, expansion, stateful preprocessing, costs, negative cases and deployment boundaries. Dictionary attack credit was checked one lab at a time against committed source and retained for ten learner-executed searches: Search Vault, Dedup Vault, Phantom Vault, Bcrypt Forge, Commit Gate, OPAQUE Gate, PSI Gate, PAKE Gate, SPAKE Gate and DNSSEC Chain. Search Vault's imported `bls` module alias does not implement a BLS signature, its mutation-test default does not execute a cryptographic fault, and the noble module filename does not turn ristretto255 into Ed25519; all three false credits are excluded. Quantum Vault's only dictionary match remains an explicitly out-of-scope threat. Commit Gate's live P-256 point arithmetic remains implementation credit, while its expressly non-RFC 9380 try-and-increment teaching map remains comment-only. Teaching-scope mutations now fail if Envelope KMS loses its HMAC anchor or Merkle comparison, shifts the anchor to a comment or regains excluded attack credit; if OXT, ristretto255, HMAC-SHA-512, PEKS or active-guessing evidence disappears; if a Search Vault false positive returns; if any positive dictionary credit or Dedup's message-locked implementation disappears; or if the Quantum threat-only mention is promoted to an executed attack.

## Progress definitions

| Stage | Evidence required |
| --- | --- |
| **Planned** | The scope and next action are recorded; implementation has not started. A named topic in a plan provides no implementation credit. |
| **In progress** | Design or implementation is active. Local implementation can be complete while integration checks or publication remain pending. State which sub-scope is active. |
| **Verified** | The stated acceptance criteria have been checked at a recorded commit, with the relevant command results and observed learner behavior. Remaining limits are explicit. This does not establish merge or deployment. |
| **Merged** | The reviewed change is present on the intended main branch, with its merge commit and completed applicable checks recorded. If main moved, affected generated checks have been rerun. |
| **Live** | Deployment revision matches the intended main commit and served bytes/learner behavior contain the change. A green workflow or push alone is insufficient. |

Use these stages for the rollout’s work. The four resolved checks below describe pre-existing catalog coverage, not newly implemented or runtime-verified achievements. Do not advance an entire proposal because its first sub-exercise passes.

| Iron Letter | 5G identity tab with bounded IMSI-based SUCI Profile A concealment and visible routing fields. | [f0fed901fc32](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-iron-letter/tree/f0fed901fc32852d74e6054fbe571bba4e666e15) | Bounded IMSI-based SUCI Profile A teaching; not a complete 5G stack, Profile B or universal tracking protection. |
| Prime Witness | Lucas–Pratt certificates | [271adc969b97](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-prime-witness/tree/271adc969b9793eac7472dae90a99a9a57e77560) | Recursive small-prime Lucas–Pratt certificates; not ECPP/AKS or certification of the separate 1024-bit probable-prime output. |
| Hidden Bit | Multi-key rare-signal game | [5c74e5bf8a18](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-hidden-bit/tree/5c74e5bf8a1826bc2bc60ac42559eec0033f2da0) | Abstract multi-key rare-signal game; not a new encryption construction or a measured proof for AES. |

**S05 is withdrawn and is not one of the 48 proposals.** Key Exchange already demonstrates commitment-based short-code device pairing, including a late-choice collision and commit-first rejection. [Pinned pairing implementation](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab-key-exchange/blob/95121cd4939143bc30604ea2db2bd2510727a606/src/pairing.ts#L31). The separate distance-bounding proposal S06 remains because authentication and physical proximity have different claims.

## Phase guide

Phases express a sensible teaching/build sequence, not promised dates. Recheck the target lab before starting each proposal; newer work may resolve or narrow it.

| Phase | Proposals | Priority | Current state |
| --- | ---: | --- | --- |
| Phase 1 — Stateful key-handle policy | 1 | P1 | 1 live |
| Phase 2 — Privacy, storage and query integrity | 11 | P1 | 11 live |
| Phase 3 — Protocols and arithmetic | 5 | P1 | 4 live; 1 planned |
| Phase 4 — Construction and cryptanalysis depth | 16 | P2 | 9 live; 7 planned |
| Phase 5 — Advanced privacy and optional workshop depth | 8 | P2 | 2 live; 6 planned |
| Phase 6 — Optional specialist and research scope | 7 | P3 | 7 planned |

### Coordination and optional scope

- L-R-05 and A-D-10 are delivered through one preprocessing/stateful PIR project with separate acceptance criteria. L-R-04 adds query compression and keeps server work separate from communication.
- Search extensions A-D-02, L-R-07, E-K-08 and L-R-08 share the Search Vault baseline, but have distinct roles, query semantics and leakage.
- A-D-06 teaches classical AES faulty-output recovery before E-K-06’s accepted-output selection leakage. Existing lattice faults and AES layer ablations remain credited.
- A-D-03 and L-R-03 share Attribute Gate but remain separate executable claims: FABESA partial policy hiding, RW15 independent authorities/GID binding and the application-layer epoch adaptation are not one combined construction or theorem. E-K-02’s actual descendant key delegation is a different interface.
- E-K-04 owns Hidden Bit's guided-hybrid scaffold: every adjacent hop identifies an exact rewrite, assumption-based replacement or bounded bad event and accumulates the final advantage. S09 is separately live through two challenge-order state machines, exact finite counts and a separating shifted-pair strategy; neither implementation supplies the other's credit.
- S03 and E-K-05 are explicitly optional P2 workshop extensions. Their existing hub workshops already execute basic timestamping and direct/ideal-intermediary fair exchange.
- All seven Phase 6 proposals are optional specialist/research scope. Implementation must first select a faithful primary construction and precise model; a conceptual version must be labeled conceptual.

### Additional source-review follow-ups — outside the 48-proposal denominator

These source-informed gaps were confirmed against fresh repository main branches on October 24, 2026. One is **Live** and four remain **Planned, unprioritized follow-ups**; they do not change the accepted proposal queue, the 48 accepted encyclopedia proposal IDs or the phase counts. `RR-*` is a local reading-review identifier, not an encyclopedia-audit ID. Existing mechanics named in each row must be reused rather than rebuilt.

The separate [paper-review implementation checklist](paper-review-extensions.md) records six 2026-10-25 paper-derived decisions without changing this queue or denominator. PR-03's bounded weak/repaired Groth16 relation, PR-05's bounded Off-Key adaptation, PR-06's exact abort/retry lesson and PR-10's quantitative FRI correction are live. PR-04 remains explicitly deferred for missing CKKS public-key/CoeffToSlot/Galois-rotation prerequisites; the lab's existing relinearization key switch is not that missing path. PR-11 is planned and contributes prior-aware controls to RR-POWER-MEASURED without adding a deep-learning stack. [Hub PR #291](https://github.com/systemslibrarian/crypto-lab/pull/291) publishes their exact reviewed pins, exhibit maps and corrected catalog scope.
